845 entries, storm-linear to story-release-package. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **storm-linear**: Execute tracker operations against Linear under the phase-split authority contract - publish a story spec to its issue, open/close execution state, comment completion records, slice child tickets, and answer "which system owns this status" questions. Use from the explicit storm-build authoring and implementation routes whenever a workflow needs to touch Linear. ([sjennings/bmad-storm](https://github.com/sjennings/bmad-storm/tree/HEAD/skills/storm-linear))
- **storm-orchestrate**: Coordinate multi-lane work with Storm specialists inside the shipped plan and execute facets. ([sjennings/bmad-storm](https://github.com/sjennings/bmad-storm/tree/HEAD/skills/storm-orchestrate))
- **storm-outline**: Run STORM phase 2 — outline generation.
